# In pursuance of Regulation 18 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1988, it is hereby notified that in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 20 1 C of the Chartered Accountants Act,

NOTIFICATION New Delhi, the 26th July, 2022 (CHARTERED ACCOUNTANTS) 3CCA (4) (1) /2018-2019.—In pursuance of Regulation 18 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1988, it is hereby notified that in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 20(1) (C) of the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, the Council of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India has removed the names of the following persons from the Register of Members of this Institute with effect from 01/10/2018 on account of non-payment of prescribed fees:Sl.
